(2) ENGINE SEAMS, each never taken in Classic (the golden Classic transcript is unchanged in both bitnesses): offense plays 8 (Sneak), 9 (Keeper) and 10 (Draw) go from `snap_phrase` to `qb_run_play`; `Rules.instead_of_sack` may answer the constant SCRAMBLE (before the grounding draw) and the new `Rules.instead_of_incompletion` is asked right after the completion draw and before the throw sentence (ClassicRules: None and False, no draw); `Rules.qb_entry`, `qb_narrate`, `qb_slide` (ClassicRules raises: never reached) and `computer_offense` (a no-op); `PlayEngine.ran()` is the one test of "this play ends as a run" (plays 1 and 2, a kick-off return, any play with `qb_run` set: before SX.5 the touchdown and the tackle branch tested the play number, and a number test was wrong for a scramble, whose number is a pass's); the menu loop lets an item have the digit 0 (no Classic item has one, the key 0 is echoed as before); `DEFENSE_MATCH` replaces the dictionary inside `offense_chosen`; `GameState` and `PlayOutcome` carry `qb_run` and `slid` (cleared with the record). (3) THE MENU (design 10.4): items 8 Quarterback Sneak, 9 Quarterback Keeper, 10 Quarterback Draw (key 0) after Punt, then Time Out (T, item 11) and Save Game (S, item 12); with the runs off the menu is the one of SX.2 to SX.4; the two-point try keeps items 1 to 5 and the defense menu has no new item. (4) THE ROWS (design 7.1, start values kept): sneak 0 to 2, 0 to 3, 1 to 4, -1 to 3, 1 to 4, 1 to 5 yards; keeper -2 to 6, -3 to 5, -4 to 3, -3 to 15, 3 to 12, 4 to 14; draw -1 to 3, -2 to 3, 1 to 6, 4 to 16, 2 to 9, 3 to 12; the scramble 1 to 14; fumbles 1 in 100, 90, 90 and 200; out of bounds 0, 12, 5 and 34 percent; yards are the original's `Int(Rnd * (most - fewest + 1)) + fewest`. A loss on a keeper is a rush for a loss, never a sack; a fumble is a fumble lost and is not counted as a run (Q60). (5) THE WORDS (design 7.2): the snap sentence, then for a keeper or a draw one line of pool QB (QB01 to QB05, the quarterback's name, never one of the last three, spoken with say4 so F2 replays it; F8 does not silence it, and with every QB line switched off the default lines speak: the play-by-play always has its sentence), the run sentence (NXQ-03, NXQ-04 with one of the original's End Run directions 4 to 7, NXQ-05), the original's own tackle phrases and sounds (sneak foot4, keeper and draw foot6) and the gain or loss sentence; a scramble keeps the drop-back sentence, then NXQ-01, " AND IS CAUGHT BY " and foot5. A sneak has no pool line (its own sentence says it all). (6) THE SCRAMBLE (design 7.1): when the original's draw gives a sack, one more draw `below(100) < 22` turns it into a scramble; when the completion draw gives an incompletion, `below(100) < 6` before the throw sentence; the pass the original had counted (`pass_attempts` and the interception count) is taken back and the scramble is a running play with its yards. (7) THE SLIDE (design 7.3) is decided where the yards are drawn (after the fumble test and the yards, before the tackle sentence), not after the narration end point as design 15 says, because it changes the yards and takes the tackle sentence's place: a gain of 8 or more yards that does not reach the goal line slides with `rnd(2) = 1`, a draw slides too, the cut is `below(3)`, the tackle sentence is " <quarterback> slides feet first " with the whistle (foot11), the run is never out of bounds and the clock keeps running (7-2-1 (d)); with penalties on, `below(40) = 0` is a late hit by the defense (P18, 15 yards, automatic first down, 12-2-8 (d)), kept as `PenaltyFlow.late` and folded in at the end of the play as the only flag or the second flag of the other team (the double-foul rules decide), never a second flag of the same team (14-1-3), and, like every foul after the play, never drawn on a two-point try or when the offense does not keep the ball (the review's findings 1 to 3). (8) THE COMPUTER (design 7.4): on third or fourth down with 1 or 2 yards to go a sneak with `rnd(2) = 1` in place of a drawn play of 1 to 5 (a kick stays a kick), otherwise on downs 1 to 3 one draw `rnd(150)`: 1 a keeper, 2 a draw; never in a two-point try; its defense matches a repeated sneak with Goal Line, a keeper with Long Run, a draw with Short Run. (9) STATISTICS (C7): every sneak, keeper, draw and scramble that ends as a run (a touchdown run, a safety and a two-point try run too) adds to `qb_runs` and `qb_yards` at the narration end point, spoken after the time outs (NXS-08, NXS-09); an accepted foul that erases the play takes the counts back. (10) TEXTS, MENU, FILES: 237 texts (five new rows of the TSV, NXH-18 to NXH-22, the help of F5, F6 and F7; NXH-22, the late hit, only with Penalties on; QB03 reads " <quarterback> pulls the ball down and runs "); the F9 Rules menu is 1 Rules, 2 Time outs, 3 Quarterback runs, 4 Onside kicks, 5 Onside question, 6 Penalties, 7 Reset; remarks.txt gets pool QB (an older file without QB lines uses the defaults); `clone/CUSTOMIZE.md` says so. (11) CALIBRATION (design 7.1 start values, B-X39): `work/calibrate_qb_sx5.py`, 10,000 seeded games with both sides played by the computer (`work/calibrate_qb_sx5_10000.txt`): designed runs 1.35 per team per game (band 1.0 to 1.6, NFL 1.29), their mean 3.25 yards (band 3.2 to 4.2, NFL 3.66; 0.59 sneaks at 1.92 yards, 0.38 keepers at 4.03 and 0.37 draws at 4.55 per team per game), scrambles 1.71 per team per game and 5.35 percent of the drop-backs (design about 5.4) at 7.25 yards (NFL 7.67), a designed run on third or fourth and 1 converted 84.2 percent (NFL 83.4), slides 49.9 percent of the 19,724 eligible gains with a mean cut of 0.99 yard, late hits 2.6 percent of the slides (rule 1 in 40); the earlier bands still hold with quarterback runs on (12.71 accepted fouls a game, 33.76 counted plays per quarter, quarters 2 and 4 carry 6.92 more, 26.66 seconds per counted play, 3.72 timeouts per team, 0.171 onside kicks a game, no property problem). Two moves from the start values, each from the counts of the engine's own plays: the sack becomes a scramble with 22 percent (the start value 15 gave 3.15 percent of the drop-backs against the design's 5.4), and the computer's keeper and draw chance is 1 in 150 each (the start value 1 in 30 gave 3.79 designed runs per team per game against the NFL's 1.29: the engine plays more downs per game than the draw was sized for). The mean yards of the designed runs sits at the low end of its band because the computer sneaks (1.92 yards) more often than the NFL's mix: a plausibility gap, kept. (12) TESTS: `test_nx_qb.py` (68: the rows of the design and their hand-worked means, the menu and its keys, Classic's no-ops, the settings, the pool and remarks.txt, the statistics, the help and the saved record), `test_nx_qb_plays.py` (84: every cell's fewest and most yards, every sentence, sound and draw of a sneak, keeper, draw and scramble on a strict scripted source, the slide's three cuts, out of bounds, a loss, a touchdown, a safety, a fumble, the computer's two rules and the defense's matching), `test_nx_qb_penalties.py` (19: the late hit accepted and declined, no second flag, an erased run taken back, the snap's foul draw for plays 8 to 10), `test_nx_qb_games.py` (11: twelve simulated games' properties and five whole games through the menus with a player who runs the quarterback), `nflrig.py` (the rig's offense calls `computer_offense`), the Rules-menu and remarks tests changed for the new item and pool; 2294 passed, 0 failed in 64-bit and in 32-bit with numpy blocked (`work/suite_sx5_64.txt`, `_32.txt`), the real-thread file test_app_startup.py ran 30 times per bitness on the final code, 30 of 30 passed in each (`work/repeat_sx5/`); the golden Classic transcript passes unchanged in both bitnesses. L. games with the runs off identical to the commit before SX.5: every text, sound, score and the next random number); no exception in 4,000 simulated games over the 16 switch combinations; the quarterback counts equal the running counts for every run, also an erased one; 2 MAJOR, 3 MINOR and 3 NOTE findings, all real, all fixed with a test where it is code: (a) a late hit on a slide during a two-point try raised KeyError(0) in the enforcement, and (b) after a fourth-down slide that fell short it was enforced for the team that fouled: one cause, the hit was queued outside the penalty flow without its conditions (B-P58); it is now drawn only when the offense keeps the ball and not on a try; (c) the hit was dropped when the offense's own foul waited: it is now folded in as the second flag of the other team and the double-foul rules decide; stale numbers and comments, the remarks.txt header, the help promising a foul with Penalties off (NXH-22 now), QB03 repeating the snap sentence, NXH-18's 'play menu' and the Classic stub's signature. `work/review_sx5_findings.md`.
